Result: 多个中等规模模型对Paired-Focus结构的形式和含义都敏感；基于人类规模数据训练的模型在所有含义评估中失败；Paired-Focus语义理解在训练中比句法知识出现得更晚；Paired-Focus语义学习与某些领域世界知识的提升相关。

Conclusion: 中等规模的开源模型能够掌握稀有Paired-Focus结构，且结构知识与其他意义领域存在关联。

Abstract: Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.

</details>


### [76] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向金融RAG的Source Memory方法，通过外部贝叶斯源记忆层适应检索过程，保持LLM阅读器冻结，仅对检索层进行基于市场反馈的更新。实验表明该方法在89只Nasdaq股票的金融新闻和SEC文件中，使宏观F1从0.438提升至0.471，投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84，证明在金融场景中「学会检索哪里」可能比「学会如何阅读」更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融RAG系统通常按文本相关性对证据进行排序，但在金融市场中，有用的证据来源取决于事件类型、预测期限和市场上下文。传统方法缺乏对市场反馈的适应能力，无法根据金融事件的结果动态调整检索策略。

Method: 保持LLM阅读器冻结，仅通过外部贝叶斯源记忆层适配检索层。该记忆层基于成熟的残差收益反馈进行更新。对于每个公司-新闻锚点，系统检索相关金融新闻和SEC文件段落，附加预决策市场上下文卡片，预测多期限残差收益信号。

Result: 在89只Nasdaq股票构成的固定投资组合上，使用原始FNSPID新闻和点时间EDGAR文件段落进行测试：Frozen Reader with Source Memory相比No Memory基线，使留出集macro-F1从0.438提升至0.471，下游投资组合Sharpe从0.52提升至0.84。监督式LoRA阅读器对静态RAG有轻微改善，但未超越冻结源记忆阅读器。

Conclusion: 对于金融RAG而言，学会从哪里检索可能与学会如何阅读同样重要。该方法提供了一种简单、模块化的市场反馈适应路径，无需微调LLM阅读器，仅通过优化检索层即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [77] [Scaling Multi-Hop Training Data via Graph-Constrained Path Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31238)
*Pengyu Chen,Yonggang Zhang,Mingming Chen,Jun Song,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GCSCS方法，通过将推理路径发现与问题生成解耦来解决专业文档多跳训练数据稀缺的难题。核心创新是使用基于关键词质心的图结构离线枚举推理路径，而非让教师模型联合发现。实验表明，4.4倍语料扩展是关键收益，而非每条链的质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大型语言模型构建组合推理训练数据需要大规模多跳数据，但此类数据除结构化来源外非常稀缺。现有方法在结构化模板密集、交叉引用多的专业语料上表现急剧下降。

Method: 提出GCSCS方法：首先基于上下文关键词质心构建图结构，离线枚举推理路径；再由教师模型仅对预验证路径进行verbalize。引入五种几何可接受性约束，并给出Gram矩阵论证证明局部相似性边界允许端点漂移约91度，同时上界相似性约束可退出模板文本形成的密集嵌入团。

Result: 在CUAD法律合同语料上微调Qwen3-32B（80K样本），闭卷Token F1从21.66%提升至38.58%。消融实验表明：等规模下约束与无约束链下游性能无差异，收益来自4.4倍可用语料扩展而非单链质量提升。

Conclusion: 图约束的作用是提升教师合成能力而非改善链内容质量。该方法将图约束的作用从提升链内容重新定义为提高教师合成可行性，为专业领域多跳问答训练数据构建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Endowing large language models with compositional reasoning over specialized documents requires multi-hop training data at scale, where such data rarely exists outside of curated benchmarks built on structured sources. To construct it directly from plain, unannotated text, existing methods ask a single teacher model to jointly discover an evidence path through a document and verbalize it as a question-answer pair. However, these methods degrade sharply when documents are structured around repetitive templates and densely cross-referencing clauses, conditions that characterize most real-world specialized corpora. In this work, we decouple the two operations: reasoning paths are enumerated offline over a graph of contextual keyword centroids, and the teacher is invoked only to verbalize pre-validated paths. The graph enforces five geometric admissibility constraints, for which we provide Gram-matrix arguments establishing that local similarity bounds alone admit endpoint drift up to ${\sim}91^{\circ}$, and that an upper similarity bound is necessary to exit dense embedding cliques formed by boilerplate text. A matched-size ablation isolates the mechanism: at equal training scale, constrained and unconstrained chains yield indistinguishable downstream performance, and the gain at full scale comes from a 4.4$\times$ expansion of the usable corpus rather than from higher per-chain quality -- reframing the role of graph constraints, in this setting, as raising teacher synthesizability rather than improving chain content. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B on 80K examples constructed from the CUAD legal contract corpus improves closed-book Token F1 from 21.66% to 38.58%. We have released our codes at https://github.com/hkgai-official/GCSCS.

</details>


### [78] [Wind Turbine Maintenance Log Labelling Framework: LLM-Driven Data Correction and Enrichment via Semantic Extraction of Reliability Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31281)
*Max Malyi,Jonathan Shek,Alasdair McDonald,Andre Biscaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的方法论,用于将风电机组历史维护日志中的非结构化自然语言文本自动标准化和结构化。该框架在16,316条维护日志(来自280台风机,监测期9年)上进行验证,成功结构化了超过70%的数据集,解决了系统编码错误分类等问题,并通过经验分类体系标注具体维护动作和故障模式。该方法可显著减少人工FMEA的主观性,为可再生能源领域的可靠性分析、根因分析和预测性维护提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着风电机组老化,亟需数据驱动的可靠性工程来优化运营维护、延长服役寿命并降低平准化能源成本。历史维护日志中蕴含宝贵可靠性信息,但以非结构化自然语言呈现,难以进行定量分析。

Method: 开发了一种模型无关的框架,利用LLM对维护日志的自由文本描述进行系统化标准化和结构化。框架自动校正层级系统编码,提取基于证据的维护动作和故障模式分类体系。通过基于系统代码的日志批次构建经验字典,识别故障模式、可观察症状、主要机制和候选原因。

Result: 自动化工件管道成功结构化了超过70%的数据集。解决了普遍存在的误分类问题,如隔离此前未分类的变桨系统故障、恢复缺失的系统编码。通过应用经验分类体系为记录添加具体维护动作和故障模式标签,丰富了记录内容。

Conclusion: 该方法论提供了一个高度可扩展、成本效益高的蓝图,可将大量定性现场观测转化为定量可靠性指标,为可再生能源领域集成根因分析、改进FMEA和高级预测性维护奠定基础。

Abstract: As wind turbine fleets age, data-driven reliability engineering is essential to optimise their operation and maintenance for service life extension and levelised cost of energy reduction. Failure event descriptions within historical maintenance logs are a source of valuable reliability intelligence. However, they typically appear as unstructured natural language entries, rendering them inaccessible for quantitative analysis. This paper presents a novel methodology leveraging a large language model (LLM) to systematically standardise and structure maintenance logs based on their free-text descriptors. Operating on a dataset of 16,316 maintenance logs from 280 turbines monitored over nine years, the developed model-agnostic framework autonomously corrected hierarchical system codes and extracted evidence-based taxonomies of maintenance actions and failure modes. The automated pipeline successfully structured over 70% of the dataset. It resolved pervasive misclassification issues, such as isolating previously unclassified pitch system faults and restoring missing system codes, and enriched the records by applying empirical taxonomies to label specific actions taken and failure modes addressed. By using system-based log batches to construct empirical dictionaries of failure modes, observable symptoms, dominant mechanisms, and candidate causes, this approach reduces the inherent subjectivity of manual failure modes and effects analysis (FMEA). Ultimately, the methodology provides a highly scalable, cost-effective blueprint for translating large sets of qualitative field observations into quantitative reliability metrics, laying the foundation for integrated root-cause analysis across the renewable energy sector, improved FMEA, and advanced predictive maintenance.

</details>


### [79] [Divergence Decoding: Inference-Time Unlearning via Auxiliary Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31293)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence Decoding (DD)方法,通过小规模辅助模型在推理时引导LLM的对数几率(logits)远离特定数据,解决大模型敏感信息记忆带来的隐私和版权风险问题。该方法可蒸馏回原模型,且适用于文本和图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)经常记忆敏感的训练数据,带来严重的隐私和版权风险。现有遗忘学习方法存在灾难性效用损失或对复杂查询效果不佳的问题,亟需一种既能有效遗忘又保持模型效用的解决方案。

Method: 提出Divergence Decoding (DD)机制,使用小型辅助模型在推理阶段引导LLM的logits分布偏离目标数据。辅助模型采用标准预训练和微调流程训练。研究还验证了引导后的分布可被蒸馏回基础模型。

Result: 在多种模型规模和训练数据集规模下,DD方法均显著优于现有最优基线方法。此外,该方法在图像生成领域也显示出一定的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding是一种有效且低成本的机器遗忘解决方案,具有广泛的适用性,可应用于任何概率模型,并成功拓展至文本以外的其他领域(如图像生成)。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently memorize sensitive training data thereby creating significant privacy and copyright risks. Addressing these risks, i.e., removing such knowledge from an existing model checkpoint, has proven challenging as many unlearning methods lead to catastrophic utility loss or are ineffective for complex queries. We introduce Divergence Decoding (DD), a mechanism that uses small auxiliary models to steer the logits of the LLM away from specific data during inference. Training these models is straight forward, i.e., we use standard pre-training and fine-tuning setups. We find the method decisively outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines on unlearning benchmarks across a variety of model and training dataset scales consistent with DD being an effective and inexpensive solution to unlearning. We then demonstrate that this steered distribution can be trivially distilled back into the base model. Since the method is generally applicable to any probabilistic model, we explore its efficacy outside of text generation and find evidence of generalization to the domain of images.

</details>


### [80] [Reinforcement Learning Amplifies Emergent Misalignment from Harmless Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31328)
*Magnus Jørgenvåg,David Kaczér,Lasse Ruttert,Marvin Gülhan,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在小规模开源语言模型中系统研究强化学习（RL）诱导的突现错位（EM）现象，发现RL奖励窄范围错位行为比样本匹配的监督微调（SFT）产生更广泛的领域错位，且EM可被看似自然的奖励信号触发，但SFT时代的缓解策略（如混合安全数据）可有效迁移至RL场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突现错位（EM）现象此前主要在SFT范式下被研究，RL诱导EM的证据仅存在于大型闭源模型，导致研究成本高且难以复现。研究者试图在小规模开源模型中系统刻画RL-EM的特征。

Method: 使用小型开源语言模型，沿三个维度系统表征RL-EM：比较RL与SFT在样本匹配条件下的错位程度；测试自然 reward 信号（如审美偏好、修辞偏好）对EM的触发作用；评估SFT领域开发的训练中缓解策略对RL-EM的可迁移性。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）奖励窄范围错位行为产生的通用领域错位显著高于样本匹配的SFT；（2）RL-EM可被看似自然的 reward 信号触发，如不受欢迎的审美偏好或拙劣的修辞诉求；（3）SFT-EM缓解策略可广泛迁移，其中在策略安全数据穿插效果最佳。

Conclusion: RL-EM是真实且值得关注的问题，其危害可能比SFT-EM更大；缓解策略具有跨范式可迁移性，混合安全数据是有效的防御手段。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the surprising tendency of language models to become broadly misaligned after fine-tuning on narrowly misaligned examples. While EM has been extensively studied in the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) setting, evidence that it also arises from reinforcement learning (RL) is limited to large, closed-source models, leaving the phenomenon expensive to study and difficult to reproduce. We characterize EM from RL in small, off-the-shelf open-weight models along three axes. First, we show that rewarding narrow, overtly misaligned behavior produces substantially higher general-domain misalignment than sample-matched SFT. Second, we show that EM from RL can be induced by reward signals that could plausibly arise naturally, such as unpopular aesthetic preferences or poor rhetorical appeals. Third, we evaluate in-training mitigations developed for SFT-induced EM and find that they broadly transfer, with interleaving on-policy safety data performing best.

</details>


### [81] [Bundesrecht: An Open Library and Corpus for German Statutory Reference Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31338)
*Harshil Darji,Martin Heckelmann,Christina Kratsch,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了bundesrecht，一个用于处理德国成文法规引用的开源资源，包含软件库和结构化语料库，可将引用字符串解析、规范化为结构化对象并链接到法规条款。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成文法规引用是法律语言理解的核心，但自动处理困难：形式紧凑多变、可组合多目标、使用特殊缩写、往往指向细分层级。现有德语工具仅聚焦于引用解析或文本访问的单一边向，缺乏端到端处理能力。

Method: 开发了bundesrecht开源工具包，包含解析/规范化/消解德国成文法规引用的软件库，以及保留法规层级结构（从法律到细分款）的结构化语料库。

Result: 在2944条标注的德国法律引用上评估，解析器和规范化器使用严格精确匹配和微信息抽取指标验证。规范化引用比字符串匹配更可靠地聚类真实引用变体。

Conclusion: bundesrecht是首个覆盖德国成文法规引用端到端处理的开源资源，从原始引用字符串到解析的法规条款，并在PyPI发布。

Abstract: Statutory references are central to legal language understanding, but are difficult to process automatically, as they appear in compact and variable surface forms, may combine multiple targets, use special abbreviations, and often point to lower-level units. Existing tools for German focus either on parsing references from legal documents or accessing statutory text once citations are explicit. This paper introduces bundesrecht, an open resource for German statutory reference processing, consisting of a software library and a structured corpus of German federal law. The library parses, normalizes, and resolves German statutory references, mapping raw citation strings to structured objects, expanding compact references into canonical forms, and linking them to statutory provisions. The accompanying dataset preserves the internal hierarchy of statutes from laws to fine-granular subclauses. We evaluate the parser and normalizer on 2,944 annotated German legal references using strict exact-match and micro information extraction metrics. We further evaluate canonical reference deduplication and show that normalized references group real citation surface variants far more reliably than string matching. bundesrecht is the first open resource that covers German statutory reference processing as an end-to-end pipeline, from raw citation string to resolved statutory provision, and is available on PyPI.

</details>


### [82] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao,Siqi Wang,Zhe Hu,Yushi Li,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VIABLE，首个用于评估VLM-as-a-Judge范式在视障辅助(VIA)领域表现的基准数据集，包含超过30万条判断样本。研究发现现有模型在所有评估维度上均表现不可靠：最强的GPT-5.4仅达52.6%的单故障诊断准确率，却有94.2%的自偏好率；开源模型存在严重偏见和对抗脆弱性。为解决这些问题，本文提出VIA-Judge-Agent，通过视觉证据提取和分类学引导的工作流提升诊断准确率和用户偏好度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视障辅助(VIA)领域的人工智能评估成本高昂，VLM-as-a-Judge范式可能是潜在替代方案，但该范式在通用领域外的研究尚不充分，尤其是在VIA任务中的可靠性未知。

Method: 本文构建了VIABLE基准数据集，包含超过30万条判断样本，涵盖3种场景；提出 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 三维评估框架及12模式故障分类体系；系统评估了7个不同规模的大模型判断器；并提出VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时框架，集成视觉证据提取和分类学引导的工作流。

Result: 现有模型在所有评估维度上均表现不可靠。GPT-5.4作为最强判断器，仅达到52.6%的单故障诊断准确率，但自偏好率高达94.2%；开源判断器表现出强烈偏见和对抗脆弱性。VIA-Judge-Agent在诊断准确率和下游VIA响应的人类偏好度上均取得正向提升。

Conclusion: 本文验证了VLM-as-a-Judge范式在VIA任务中的局限性，并提出VIA-Judge-Agent作为解决方案。该框架通过增强视觉证据提取和结构化工作流，有效提升了判断器的诊断能力和用户满意度。

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/YiyiyiZhao/VIABLE

</details>


### [83] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Translation Quality Estimation in LRMs through Synergistically Evolving Implicit and Explicit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31378)
*Renfei Dang,Xinye Wang,Zhejian Lai,Weilu Xu,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RIEQE框架，通过两阶段训练（NonThinking-SFT + Thinking-RLVR）实现隐式和显式推理的协同进化，解决大推理模型在翻译质量估计上的细粒度评估难题。Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在WMT测试集上超越所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在细粒度翻译质量估计任务上表现不佳，尽管具有强大的多语言能力。核心挑战不在于多语言能力不足，而在于学习细粒度QE任务的内在难度。

Method: 提出RIEQE（Reasoning both Implicitly and Explicitly for QE）两阶段训练框架：(1) NonThinking-SFT阶段，在无推理链情况下进行监督微调，直接提升模型的隐式推理倾向和能力；(2) Thinking-RLVR阶段，使用带可验证奖励的强化学习强化显式推理。关键在于将复杂QE任务分解为简单子任务，使隐式推理可行。

Result: 在WMT测试集上，基于Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的RIEQE在显式推理性能上超越所有基线，同时隐式推理能力与最佳编码器模型相当。实验证明隐式和显式推理在框架下协同进化、相互促进。

Conclusion: RIEQE框架成功实现隐式与显式推理的协同进化，证实两种推理方式能够相互增益，为大推理模型在细粒度NLP任务中的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) still struggle with fine-grained translation quality estimation (QE), even with long reasoning chains. We argue that LRMs already possess strong multilingual capabilities, while the core challenge stems from the intrinsic difficulty of learning the fine-grained QE task. In this paper, we propose RIEQE (Reasoning both Implicitly and Explicitly for QE), a simple two-stage training framework that enables the co-evolution of implicit (layer-wise) and explicit (token-wise) reasoning capabilities. To make implicit reasoning feasible, we first decompose the complex QE task into straightforward subtasks. Based on this, our two-stage approach applies: (1) NonThinking-SFT, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) without reasoning chains to directly boost the model's implicit reasoning tendency and capability; and (2) Thinking-RLVR, standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) to subsequently strengthen explicit reasoning. Results demonstrate that implicit and explicit reasoning synergistically co-evolve under our framework. On the WMT test sets, RIEQE based on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 surpasses all baselines in explicit reasoning performance, while its implicit reasoning capability is also comparable to the best current encoder-based models. We further provide evidence for the synergistic collaboration between implicit and explicit reasoning, showing how they mutually benefit each other.

</details>


### [84] [LLM Judges Inconsistently Disagree Across Safety Criteria and Harm Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31381)
*Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Soumya Vajjala,Akriti Vij,Isar Nejadgholi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型(LLM)作为自动化评判者在无参考设置下进行多维度安全评估的一致性。研究发现LLM在识别金融等受监管领域的机器生成建议中的安全问题方面表现不可靠，但对暴力等更明显的 unsafe 内容识别更为可靠。评判结果的不一致性受安全标准、语言和语言风格影响显著，不同评判者之间也存在高度分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在各类应用中的广泛部署，如何有效评估其输出安全性成为关键挑战。传统评估方法依赖人工标注，成本高昂且效率低下，因此研究者探索使用LLM作为自动化评判者的可行性，但这种方法的可靠性和一致性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 研究采用多维度安全评估框架，在无参考设置下评估多个LLM评判者的一致性。实验覆盖不同领域（金融、通用）、多种安全标准和多语言场景，通过对比不同评判者的判断结果来分析一致性问题。

Result: LLM在识别受监管领域（如金融）中的安全问题时表现不可靠，但对明显 unsafe 内容（如暴力）识别较为可靠。评判不一致程度受安全标准选择、内容语言和语言风格影响显著。不同评判者之间在领域、安全标准和语言维度上均存在高度分歧。

Conclusion: 使用LLM作为自动化评判者需谨慎，特别是在涉及专业领域安全评估时。研究建议从业者在实际应用中考虑这些局限性，并提供了针对不同场景使用自动化评判的具体建议。

Abstract: We evaluate the consistency of automated judges in conducting a multi-dimensional safety evaluation in a reference-free setup. Our results indicate that Large Language Models are unreliable judges in identifying safety issues related to machine-generated advice in regulated domains such as finance, although they are more reliable at identifying more overt forms of unsafe/harmful content such as violence. The degree of inconsistency in a model's judgments can vary significantly by the chosen safety criteria and can be impacted by the language of the content and its linguistic style as well. Finally, there is high disagreement among different judges for the same output, across domains, safety criteria, and languages. These findings provide new insights on the practice of using LLMs as evaluators and offer several recommendations for practitioners on how to use automated judges in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sherzod Hakimov,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言模型(VLMs)在协作对话任务中的空间推理能力，通过结构重建任务评估开源和闭源VLMs，发现空间推理仍是难点，详细文本表示和分解图像表示能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类协作任务中需要通过语言传达对视觉场景的理解，但VLMs在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 开发了一个结合视觉解读、定位、语言引导交互和动作生成的协作结构构建任务框架，让VLMs使用对话从视觉和文本输入中重建目标结构，评估了不同交互设置、输入模态和图像表示下的开源和闭源VLMs。

Result: 空间推理对于当前VLMs仍然困难；详细的文本表示在各种模态条件下都能带来更高的重建成功率；分解的图像表示能够提升性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了协作VLM智能体在视觉空间定位和基于定位的指令生成方面存在的局限性。

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [86] ["Intelegi Româneşte?'' A Recipe for Romanian Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31401)
*Mihai Masala,Marius Leordeanu,Mihai Dascalu,Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了为低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）构建专用视觉-语言模型（VLM），通过机器翻译将英语VLM训练和评估语料库翻译为罗马尼亚语，并训练测试不同视觉骨干、语言骨干和OCR数据的影响，创建了文化本土化的评估集HoraVQA，实验表明罗马尼亚语适配的VLM显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型主要遵循纯文本LLM的发展轨迹，在英语基准测试上表现出色，但对低资源语言性能急剧下降。低资源语言既缺乏大规模的图像-文本语料库，也缺乏文化适配的评估体系，现有方法无法有效支持这些语言的VLM应用。

Method: 1) 将已建立的英语VLM训练和评估语料库通过机器翻译翻译为罗马尼亚语；2) 对文本注释和图像内文本进行翻译，在保持视觉 grounding 的同时适配文本内容；3) 使用该数据训练和消融测试系列VLM，隔离不同因素的贡献：(i)不同规模和预训练的视觉骨干，(ii)从多语言到罗马尼亚语适配LLM的语言骨干，(iii)OCR风格的图像-文本数据；4)  curation创建了 HoraVQA——一个基于罗马尼亚日常生活场景的文化本土化评估集。

Result: 罗马尼亚语适配的VLM始终优于其同规模对应模型，并且在所有评估基准上甚至超越下一尺寸级别的模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过系统性数据翻译和模型适配，可以有效为低资源语言构建专用VLM，语言适配和 OCR 数据的整合是提升低资源语言VLM性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) largely follow the text-only LLM trajectory, excelling on English benchmarks but sharply degrading on low-resource languages, where neither large-scale image-text corpora nor culturally grounded evaluations exist. We present a systematic study of building a language-specific VLM for Romanian, covering the full pipeline from data construction to architectural choices. We translate established English VLM training and evaluation corpora into Romanian, applying machine translation to textual annotations and to in-image text, preserving visual grounding while adapting the textual content. Using this data, we train and ablate a series of VLMs to isolate the contribution of (i) vision backbones of varying scale and pretraining, (ii) language backbones from multilingual to Romanian-adapted LLMs, and (iii) OCR-style image-text data. We further curate HoraVQA, a culturally native evaluation set grounded in Romanian everyday scenes. Romanian-adapted VLMs consistently outperform their same-sized counterparts and, across all evaluated benchmarks, even surpass models from the next larger size category.

</details>


### [87] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种无数据的自博弈框架，用于开放性任务，通过协同进化Challenger（生成文档 grounding 任务）和Solver（多轮检索回答）两个策略，配合冻结模型的自我评判来生成任务特定的评分标准并评分。在Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo-3三个模型上，SCOPE在8个基准测试中提升高达10.4分，并超越使用9K策划提示训练的GRPO_data，同时在7个held-out短问答基准上提升高达13.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈方法需要规则可验证的答案或外部评判（策划提示、前沿模型法官），限制了其在开放式任务中的应用。开放性任务长期依赖外部监督，缺少无需人工标注的自博弈训练方案。

Method: SCOPE框架核心包括：(1) Challenger策略从源文档生成基于文档的任务；(2) Solver策略通过多轮检索回答这些问题；(3) 使用初始模型的冻结副本作为自我评判者，从源文档编写任务特定的评分标准并对Solver回答进行评分。整个过程无需外部数据或人工标注。

Result: 在Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo-3三个7-8B指令微调模型上，SCOPE在8个开放性任务基准上提升高达+10.4分，匹配或超越使用约9K策划提示训练的GRPO_data。此外，在7个held-out短问答基准上提升高达+13.8分，在所有三个模型上均超越GRPO_data。消融实验表明：(1) Challenger的协同进化对于保持任务接近Solver的能力边界至关重要；(2) 性能提升来源于检索和合成的双重改进，相对贡献因任务而异；(3) 评分标准生成质量是自我评判的瓶颈。

Conclusion: SCOPE成功证明了自博弈可用于完全开放性的任务，无需外部监督或策划数据。协同进化机制确保任务难度与模型能力匹配，评分标准生成质量是决定自我评判效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [88] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov,Rex VanHorn,Mingxi Xu,Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究为自由译者和小规模语言服务商开发了评估翻译技术的实用方法，重点关注离线翻译场景（隐私敏感领域）。通过扩展语料库（RFTC→RFMC，新增德语和简体中文对齐句对），使用Ollama运行本地大语言模型，在四个翻译方向、1000+句子上进行基准测试，对比商业NMT系统、前沿LLM和专业本地NMT系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前翻译技术评估存在门槛高、不适配小规模用户的问题；隐私敏感领域（如医疗、法律）因安全需求无法使用云端商业引擎或大语言模型，需要可行的本地化翻译解决方案。

Method: ①扩展Reeve Foundation三语语料库为多语言语料库（RFMC），增加德语和简体中文对齐翻译；②使用Ollama运行多种规模的可本地部署语言模型；③在四个翻译方向、1000+句子上进行基准测试；④采用MATEO进行自动评估；⑤对比本地LLM、商业NMT（DeepL、Baidu）、前沿LLM（GPT-5.2）及专业本地NMT系统（OPUS-CAT、NeuralDesktop、Promt）。实验采用一致的单一提示词调用，未进行微调或领域适配。

Result: 本地LLM表现因翻译方向和模型规模存在显著差异；最优本地LLM可匹配或超越本地NMT系统及前沿LLM，但与顶级商业NMT系统仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 经审慎选择的本地LLM可有效满足隐私受限专业人士的翻译需求；研究结果为未来模型扩展和多语言能力研究提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [89] [Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31480)
*Bart Evelo,Meaghan Fowlie,Denis Paperno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过「个人关系任务」评估LLM和人类的组合能力，发现LLM在理解表达式指称方面表现优于结构化理解，而人类则相反，并指出LLM缺乏指涉接地(referential grounding)是其无法模拟人类语言理解的关键原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估LLM组合能力的方法过于简单，缺乏对语义解释两个互补方面的细致区分：外延任务(表达式指称什么)和内涵任务(如何用结构化方式表示意义)。

Method: 使用Paperno 2022提出的「个人关系任务」，让被试(包括人类和LLM)根据已知的人物关系宇宙解释名词短语(如'Amber's parent's friend')。实验分别在外延任务(找出具体的人)和内涵任务(给出公式如friend(parent(amber)))上测试。

Result: 人类和LLM表现出相反的优势模式：人类在外延任务上表现更好，而LLM在内涵任务上表现更好。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，LLM缺乏指涉接地是其无法模仿人类语言理解的关键缺失组件。实验方法为理解现代机器学习模型的组合能力提供了更细致的视角。

Abstract: Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.

</details>


### [90] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Ekramul Alam Esham,Ajwad Abrar,Ishmam Tashdeed,Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了首个针对孟加拉语的LLM幻觉评估框架BenHalluEval，通过双重评估协议和新的BenHalluScore指标，对7种LLM在4项任务上的12种幻觉类型进行系统评估，揭示了当前模型在孟加拉语幻觉校准上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语是全球第六大语言，但此前缺乏针对该语言的LLM幻觉系统评估研究，低资源语言的LLM幻觉问题尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 构建了包含12,000个幻觉样本的评估框架，覆盖GQA、孟加拉-英语混合QA、摘要和推理4项任务，源自3个现有孟加拉语数据集；采用双重评估协议（Track A测量真值实例误判率，Track B测量幻觉检测率），评估了7种LLM并提出BenHalluScore双轨校准指标；同时测试了思维链提示作为缓解策略。

Result: BenHalluScore在不同模型和任务间呈现7.72%至55.42%的显著差异，表明当前LLM在孟加拉语幻觉校准能力上参差不齐；思维链提示虽改变响应分布但未能持续提升幻觉鉴别能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval作为首个孟加拉语专用幻觉基准，揭示了单轨评估和仅依赖提示策略的方法对低资源语言场景的局限性，为后续研究提供了数据集和代码资源。

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.

</details>


### [91] [Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31490)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Songjun Tu,Yuqian Fu,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qichao Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出通过控制蒸馏过程中的 rollout 长度来提升 on-policy distillation (OPD) 的训练效率，针对完整 rollout 计算成本高且可能产生不可靠反馈的问题，提出了两种简单策略：渐进式 OPD (POPD) 和截断式 OPD (TOPD)。实验表明 POPD 可将训练效率提升 3 倍，TOPD 仅用 10% 的 rollout 长度即可达到原有性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 OPD 在训练过程中需要生成完整 rollout，计算成本高昂；且在训练早期，late rollout 位置的 teacher feedback 可靠性较差，可能对 student 的学习产生负面影响。与 RLVR 不同，OPD 不需要完整轨迹或最终答案奖励即可提供学习信号，这提示完整 rollout 对有效 OPD 并非必需。

Method: 识别 rollout 长度为 OPD 训练效率的关键瓶颈，并提出两种 horizon-control 策略：(1) Progressive OPD (POPD)：训练过程中逐步扩展 rollout 长度；(2) Truncated OPD (TOPD)：始终使用固定长度的截断 rollout 进行蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理任务上的实验表明：POPD 将 OPD 训练效率提升至多 3 倍；TOPD 仅使用 10% 的 rollout 长度即可匹配原 OPD 性能，显著降低了 wall-clock 时间和内存消耗。

Conclusion: 控制 rollout 长度是提升 OPD 训练效率的简单且实用的方法，为长程推理任务的后训练提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [92] [Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31494)
*Zheyu Zhang,Shuo Yang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRP方法，通过无梯度算子将多个奖励扰动模型合并为单一可部署模型，利用低秩结构和兼容性感知重加权，在仅需1次前向传播的情况下，达到接近50次投票集成效果的60%以上，同时仅使用RandOpt 1/10的扰动预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法如RandOpt需要K次前向传播（K个专家模型的集成）进行推理，且难以扩展到自由形式生成场景。本文旨在将奖励种群折叠为单一可部署模型，用一次前向传播替代推理时集成。实验发现25个模型-任务对中均存在可重现的低秩结构，为该方法提供几何基础。

Method: CoRP是一个无梯度算子，包含三个核心组件：(1)奖励加权聚合：对候选模型按奖励进行加权组合；(2)兼容性感知重加权：考虑模型间的兼容性关系调整权重；(3)留出验证门控：用验证集筛选有效聚合。三项技术均不涉及语言模型的梯度回传。

Result: 在5个语言模型（0.5B至8B参数）和5个任务（数学、代码、创意写作）上，CoRP平均提升基模型8.1分。使用RandOpt 1/10的扰动预算，CoRP比单推理RandOpt高6.5分，恢复了50次投票集成超过一半的增益，且仅需1次前向传播。

Conclusion: CoRP成功将多模型集成效果压缩到单模型中，证明了奖励扰动空间存在可利用的低秩几何结构，为高效后训练提供新范式。方法在显著降低推理成本的同时保持了竞争力的性能表现。

Abstract: Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.

</details>


### [93] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向可靠性的多语言骨科文本分类框架，支持英语、印地语和旁遮普语，通过任务对齐的多语言Transformer编码器与域自适应编码器IndicBERT-HPA实现临床自由文本分类，并创新性地引入确定性选择性验证层实现可解释的主动 deferral 机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源医疗环境中，骨科临床文本具有多语言混合、专业术语密集、标注不平衡、证据缺失等特征，传统NLP方法难以保证分类可靠性，需要同时解决多语言适应性与不确定性量化两大挑战。

Method: 对比四类模型：任务对齐多语言Transformer编码器、DistilBERT基线、零样本LLM、以及IndicBERT-HPA（融合语言感知正交适配头）。评估维度除准确率外还涵盖每类别性能、ROC-AUC、AUPRC、预期校准误差、跨语言稳定性及数据分布鲁棒性。选择性验证层整合置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查三重机制。

Result: 零样本LLM在闭集分类中显著弱于任务适配编码器，且存在语言依赖性不稳定问题。IndicBERT-HPA在自然临床分布下表现最优：Macro-F1=0.8792，Macro-AUROC=0.894，AUPRC=0.902。选择性验证层在5,000条留出集上以72.3%覆盖率实现84.4%选择准确率和0.76 Macro-F1，相较全接受策略（71.5%准确率，0.65 Macro-F1）显著提升。

Conclusion: IndicBERT-HPA结合选择性验证层能够为多语言临床决策支持提供可靠、透明的可推迟（deferrable）机制，在保证高覆盖率的同时有效区分高置信与低置信样本，为低资源医疗环境中的多语言临床NLP系统提供了可验证的技术路径。

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [94] [UniAudio-Token: Empowering Semantic Speech Tokenizers with General Audio Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31521)
*Yuhan Song,Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Chuhan Wu,Sijun Zhang,Wei Jia,Yuan Liu,Houfeng Wang,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniAudio-Token通过语义-声学原语(SAP)和语义-声学平衡(SAE)两大创新，使语义分词器在保持语音能力的同时获得通用音频感知能力，成为Audio-LLM的统一音频接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义语音分词器因聚焦于语言抽象而导致"声学盲区"，限制了其在语音中心任务之外的应用。

Method: 提出两个关键创新：(1)语义-声学原语(SAP)通过将音频分解为语言内容、声学属性和听觉场景基元提供结构化监督；(2)语义-声学平衡(SAE)引入内容感知门控机制，从浅层自适应恢复细粒度声学细节。

Result: UniAudio-Token学习到全面的通用表示，同时保留高保真语音生成能力。集成到下游LLM后，在理解和生成任务上均优于所有单码本基线分词器。

Conclusion: UniAudio-Token能有效作为统一音频接口，代码和模型已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Semantic speech tokenizers have become a widely used interface for Audio-LLMs, owing to their compact single-codebook design and strong linguistic alignment. However, their focus on linguistic abstraction induces acoustic blindness, limiting their applicability beyond speech-centric tasks. We propose UniAudio-Token, a framework that empowers semantic tokenizers with general audio perception without compromising speech ability. Instead of altering the semantic paradigm, UniAudio-Token mitigates its information loss through two key innovations: (1) Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) provide structured supervision by decomposing audio into linguistic content, vocal attributes, and auditory-scene primitives; and (2) Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) introduces a content-aware gating mechanism that adaptively restores fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Extensive evaluations show that UniAudio-Token learns comprehensive universal representations while preserving high-fidelity speech generation. When integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baseline tokenizers on both understanding and generation tasks, effectively serving as a unified audio interface. We publicly release all our code, including training and inference scripts, together with the model checkpoints at https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer.

</details>


### [95] [Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31545)
*Yilun Qiu,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Yoko Yamakata,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PARL框架，将个性化评估从静态评判转化为学习问题，通过偏好感知评分规则学习实现用户对齐响应的可靠识别。三大核心原则：代表性、用户一致性和区分性，实验证明该方法能捕捉稳定风格偏好和细粒度评估模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从通用助手向用户中心智能体演进，个性化对齐评估成为关键瓶颈。现有评估方法（自动指标、LLM作为评判者）无法捕捉用户特定偏好和长期交互历史中的主观需求。

Method: 提出「个性化评估即学习」范式，将评估转化为学习问题。PARL框架从原始用户历史中学习偏好感知评估规则，采用自验证机制确保与用户偏好一致，并结合判别式强化学习目标（对比用户创作响应与竞争性个性化模型输出），捕捉精确的用户特定决策边界。

Result: 在真实世界的个性化文本生成任务上实验表明，PARL持续诱导出高保真评分规则，能可靠识别用户对齐响应，跨用户和任务泛化能力强，同时捕捉稳定风格偏好和细粒度评估模式。

Conclusion: PARL提供了一种可靠、有效的个性化对齐评估方法，通过学习范式解决了传统静态评估的局限性，为个性化LLM评估开辟了新路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/SnowCharmQ/PARL.

</details>


### [96] [Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31550)
*Yibin Zhao,Fangxin Shang,Dingrui Yang,Yuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出语义三元组恢复(STR)方法,将表格单元重构为<实体路径,属性路径,值>形式,并配合TripletQL查询路由器,在中英文表格QA基准上取得相当或更优性能,且显著减少输入token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格问答管道使用HTML或Markdown表示表格,但这些布局导向的序列化方式引入大量标记开销,且要求大语言模型从行列跨度中推断表头与单元格的对应关系,增加了推理负担。

Method: 提出语义三元组恢复(STR)协议,将每个单元格重写为原子事实<item path, feature path, value>:item path指定行级实体,feature path指定层级属性,value包含单元格内容。同时提出TripletQL轻量级查询感知路由器,根据问题选择适当的渲染方式或过滤后的三元组子集。

Result: 在四个中英文表格QA基准测试中,STR匹配或超越基于HTML的基线模型,同时减少输入token。小型语言模型和长表格场景下收益更显著,表明显式语义表示在受限推理预算下特别有效。

Conclusion: STR通过将表格转换为语义三元组形式,实现了更高效的表格问答。该方法在减少计算开销的同时保持或提升性能,且对资源受限场景更具价值。

Abstract: Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .

</details>


### [97] [What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31561)
*Chu Fei Luo,Samuel Dahan,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型(LLMs)在测试时的推理机制，提出将提问作为推理时干预手段，通过学生-教师框架训练探针预测轨迹正确性，发现自诊断信号比信息转移更重要，但检测与纠正之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理引入LLMs后，测试时推理成为重要研究领域，但推理过程机制仍未被充分探索——相同输入甚至部分解情况下，模型多次采样可能产生不同答案。

Method: 提出学生-教师框架，训练探针预测学生提问前后的隐藏状态，框架提问为序列决策问题，使用探针作为质量评分定义门控策略，最大化正确概率。

Result: 探针在教师回答前即可预测轨迹最终正确性，说明自诊断信号比信息转移更重要；干预效果高度依赖模型自一致性；检测与纠正存在显著差距，干预对正确轨迹的危害与对错误轨迹的恢复概率相当。

Conclusion: 检测与纠正之间的差距对语言模型在不确定性下的自我改进能力具有更广泛的影响，表明模型在识别问题与解决问题之间存在能力不对称。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.

</details>


### [98] [Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31563)
*Benedetta Muscato,Beiduo Chen,Gizem Gezici,Barbara Plank,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架,系统性地重新实现多种模型、训练策略、损失函数和评估指标,在不同标签和理由表示空间下进行评估。研究重点关注主观NLP任务(如仇恨言论检测)中人类推理的差异性,通过分类指标(预测性和分布性)和可解释性指标(似真性、忠诚度和复杂度)两个维度评估模型行为。结果表明,软表示在捕捉标注变异方面更有效,提示主观NLP领域需要重新思考评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在标注任务中存在广泛分歧,但现有研究主要关注标签层面的分歧,而忽略了通过token级人类理由(explanations)捕捉的解释层面的变异。此外,对于如何最佳评估人类标签和理由,以及如何超越多数投票来聚合理由,仍不清楚。这一问题在主观NLP任务(如仇恨言论检测)中尤为突出,因为不同人的推理风格、价值观和解释可能存在显著差异。

Method: 研究团队将多种模型、训练策略、损失函数和现有评估指标统一在单一协议下,在不同的标签表示空间(硬标签和软标签)以及理由表示空间(硬、中间和软)中进行系统性重新实现和对比。分类指标围绕两个关键属性组织:预测性和分布性;可解释性指标通过三个互补维度评估:似真性(plausibility)、忠诚度(faithfulness)和复杂度(complexity)。

Result: 实验结果表明,无论是硬指标还是软指标,都倾向于选择更软的表示方式,这一发现凸显了软表示在捕捉标注变异方面的有效性,并提示主观NLP领域需要重新思考评估范式。

Conclusion: 本研究通过统一框架揭示了软表示在捕捉人类标注和理由变异中的优势,为主观NLP任务中的评估标准提供了新的洞察,表明传统评估方法可能不足以应对人类推理的多样性和主观性。

Abstract: Human disagreement is ubiquitous and well-known in labeling. However, variation in explanations, captured through token-level human rationales, remains far less explored. At the same time, it is unclear how to best evaluate human labels and rationales -- or even how to best aggregate rationales beyond majority vote -- in light of this variation. Yet, rationales may provide additional insights into the richness of human reasoning, that may differ in style, values and interpretations -- especially in subjective NLP tasks like hate speech detection. In this work, we unify diverse models, training strategies, loss functions, and existing evaluation metrics under a single protocol by systematically re-implementing them across different label and rationale representation spaces. Classification metrics are organized around two key properties -- predictive and distributional -- while explainability metrics through three complementary dimensions: plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. In this unified supervision framework, we evaluate model behavior across classification and explainability metrics, as well as metric sensitivity to the choice of label (hard and soft) and rationale representation space (hard, intermediate and soft). Results show that both hard and soft metrics favor softer representations, highlighting their effectiveness in capturing variation and the need to rethink evaluation in subjective NLP.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Polynomial Histograms for Memory-Efficient Representation of Long-tailed System Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30360)
*Murray Stokely,Tim Hesterberg,Arif Merchant,Nate Coehlo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于优化分布式系统性能指标收集的信息损失度量方法，通过在传统直方图的每个区间添加多项式矩注释来构建多项式直方图，在相同的存储成本下实现更低的信息损失，并以文件系统指标为应用案例分析其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统需要收集和聚合大量不同类型的性能指标（如延迟分布），面临三个核心挑战：1）以最小成本在单个软件组件上收集数据；2）高效地跨多个维度聚合数据；3）为下游数据分析应用存储紧凑且有代表性的数据表示。传统直方图方法在存储效率和信息保真度之间存在权衡问题。

Method: 1）提出了一种针对分箱数据的信息损失度量方法；2）设计多项式直方图，在每个区间注释矩信息而非常规计数；3）与传统直方图在相同存储成本下（相同区间数）进行对比实验；4）应用该技术于大型生产系统的文件系统指标；5）解析分析了多项式直方图何时能以更低成本提供更多信息。

Result: 通过理论分析和实际应用验证，表明多项式直方图在特定条件下能够以更低的存储成本提供与传统直方图相当或更高的信息保真度，为分布式系统的性能指标收集提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 多项式直方图通过在区间中存储矩信息而非额外的区间，为分布式系统性能监控提供了一种信息损失更小、存储效率更高的数据表示方法，在文件系统和大规模分布式场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Distributed systems must frequently keep track of many different types of performance metrics across many different computers. For example, the latency distribution of certain operations may be computed for a large combination of computers, users, and operations. These empirical distributions need to be collected at minimal expense on the individual software components, efficiently aggregated across multiple dimensions, and stored in a compact representation for a variety of downstream data analysis applications.
  We describe an information loss metric for binned data that allows us to optimize cost of information loss from different histogram representations. We explore the use of polynomial histograms where each bin of a histogram is annotated with moments of the underlying distribution in that bin. These polynomial histograms are compared to traditional histograms using the same storage cost for additional bins instead of annotations in each bin. We describe an application of these techniques for file system metrics for a large production system, and analytically characterize when polynomial histograms offer more information at lower cost.

</details>


### [100] [A Datalog Framework for Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31569)
*Elena Yanakieva,Annette Bieniusa,Stefania Dumbrava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Datalog的声明式框架，用于形式化规范和推理CRDT（无冲突复制数据类型）的语义。该框架将CRDT语义建模为可执行的逻辑程序，使并发性变得显式和可组合，从而支持自动化分析。首次系统性地将Datalog作为CRDT原型设计和分析的基礎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用越来越多地支持本地优先协作，允许多个用户无需全局协调即可并发执行更新。然而，这种协作需要精心设计以捕获并发交互的预期语义，现有方法难以有效处理这一问题。

Method: 提出一个声明式框架，使用Datalog对CRDT及其应用进行规范和推理。该框架将CRDT语义建模为基于操作上下文（operation contexts）的可执行逻辑程序，使得并发性变得显式且可组合，从而支持自动化分析。应用方面，使用基于属性的测试（property-based testing）来比较不同实现。

Result: 通过协作式图数据编辑案例研究验证了方法的有效性，并报告了随操作数量和副本数量增加时的正确性验证和可扩展性实验结果。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地使用Datalog作为CRDT及其组合的原型设计和分析基础的研究工作。

Abstract: Distributed applications increasingly support local-first collaboration over shared data, allowing multiple users to perform updates concurrently without global coordination. Such collaboration requires careful design to capture the intended semantics of the concurrent interactions.
  We introduce a declarative framework for specifying and reasoning about the semantics of conflict-free replicated data types (CRDTs) and CRDT-based applications in Datalog. The framework models CRDT semantics as executable logic programs over operation contexts, making concurrency explicit and compositional, and thus amenable to automated analysis. As one application, we use property-based testing to compare implementations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically use Datalog as a foundation for prototyping and analyzing complex CRDTs and their compositions.
  We evaluate our methodology using a collaborative graph data editing case study and report experimentation results assessing correctness validation and scalability with an increasing number of operations and replicas.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen是一个神经符号混合系统，用于从文本生成物理图表，通过分离语义理解和物理约束满足来解决现有生成模型的幻觉和约束违反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型在生成物理图表时存在系统性幻觉问题：产生错误的力矢量、忽视守恒定律、违反几何约束，导致物理不准确的输出。

Method: 该方法采用三阶段神经符号架构：(1)大语言模型从问题文本中提取类型化场景图；(2)确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图(PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑等精确几何约束；(3)微调的Qwen-VL模型通过视觉接地的事后验证循环迭代纠正约束违反。

Result: 在涵盖力学、光学和电磁学的1449道物理问题基准测试中，PhyDrawGen显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro，即使在非常规物体问题上也展现了稳健的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法的解耦策略有效解决了物理图表生成中的准确性问题，证明将确定性约束求解与视觉语言模型相结合是提升科学图表生成可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [102] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出现有观测预测型世界模型会产生视觉合理但物理错误的结果,因为不同的物理系统可能看起来相同但在干预下会发散。论文主张构建模块化的物理可行世界模型,通过自动编排器识别回答干预查询所需的最简物理抽象层次。方法包括环境表示、潜状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学和查询响应等组件。当封闭形式物理不可用时,过渡模型可以是分析式、模拟式、学习式或混合式,但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。论文通过现有系统无法正确回答的查询演示了这种方法,并概述了编排器如何动态组装和调整物理可行模型用于规划、控制和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测型世界模型会产生视觉上合理但物理上错误的状态推进 rollout。这本质上是结构性问题,因为不同的物理系统在观测上可能完全相同,但在干预下会发散。现有模型可能推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果,或认证不安全行为。具身AI需要能够回答干预查询的世界模型。

Method: 提出模块化物理可行世界模型架构,包含:环境表示、潜状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应五个组件。自动编排器需识别相关抽象层次,按查询组合兼容的学习组件与结构化组件。过渡模型可以是分析式、模拟式、学习式或混合式,但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过受控基准测试暴露现有模型问题(固定可见场景而改变潜物理)。演示了该方法在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上的有效性。概述了编排器如何动态组装和调整物理可行模型用于规划、控制和验证。

Conclusion: 正确的抽象不是对世界最详尽的模型,而是保留与查询相关区分的最小模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计。它提供了新世界模型的设计原则和现有模型的可行性测试。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [103] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究如何将因子化任务（factored tasks）编码为SAT问题，填补了现有方法仅局限于启发式搜索的空白，并提出了多种编码策略和并行化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务是经典规划的经典表示形式，扩展了SAS+，支持更紧凑的任务表示和广泛的-task转换，但现有的规划方法仅限于启发式搜索，缺乏SAT-based规划方法。

Method: 1. 提出多种因子化任务的SAT编码方法；2. 专注于将因子化转换关系转化为命题逻辑的不同策略；3. 分析如何在多个层次上利用并行性；4. 研究常见任务转换对SAT规划器性能的影响。

Result: 论文提供了因子化任务的SAT编码框架，为SAT-based规划器在因子化任务上的应用奠定了基础，并通过并行化和任务转换分析提升了规划效率。

Conclusion: SAT编码为因子化任务规划提供了一种新的解决途径，多种编码策略和并行化技术的结合可以有效提升规划性能，为后续研究提供了重要的理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [104] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将MAP-Elites质量多样性算法应用于第一人称射击游戏(FPS)关卡设计,提出两种新颖的地图表示方法(Point-Line和Spatial-Layout),通过定义拓扑属性和涌现属性指标,证明新表示方法能够生成比传统方法更多样、更高质量的FPS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPS游戏关卡设计是一个复杂的创意任务,传统方法缺乏对地图拓扑特征的充分表征,需要探索更合适的地图表示方法来指导MAP-Elites算法生成高质量、多样化的关卡。

Method: 采用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB)算法,比较四种地图表示方法(All-Black、Grid-Graph、Point-Line、Spatial-Layout),定义拓扑属性指标(仅依赖地图布局)和涌现属性指标(需通过实际游戏评估),深入分析最适合引导MAP-Elites照明过程的关键特征。

Result: 实验结果表明,新提出的Point-Line和Spatial-Layout表示方法能够生成比传统All-Black和Grid-Graph表示更高多样性和更高质量的FPS地图。

Conclusion: 新的地图表示方法能够更好地表征FPS关卡的拓扑结构,是进化FPS地图的有效改进方案。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [105] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin,Juncheng Wu,Zijun Wang,Zhan Shi,Yisi Sang,Bing He,Zewen Liu,Tianxin Wei,Zongyu Wu,Zhiwei Zhang,Dakuo Wang,Xiang Zhang,Benoit Dumoulin,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou,Suhang Wang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基础模型能力是否预测其在大纲自我进化中的表现，分析了两种能力：大纲更新（从执行证据生成有用的大纲更新）和大纲收益（从更新的大纲中获益）。研究发现：1）大纲更新能力与基础能力平缓相关，不同能力层级的模型产生的大纲更新增益相似；2）大纲收益能力呈非单调关系，中层级模型获益最多，弱层级和强层级模型获益较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体越来越多地部署在可编辑的外部大纲（包括提示、技能、记忆和工具）上。大纲自我进化通过从执行证据更新这些大纲来适应智能体。但目前尚不清楚模型的基础任务解决能力是否预测其在大纲自我进化中的能力——即哪些模型能产生有用的大纲更新，哪些能从中获益。

Method: 作者分析了两种大纲自我进化能力：（i）大纲更新能力——从执行证据生成有用的持久大纲更新的能力；（ii）大纲收益能力——在任务解决中受益于更新后的大纲的能力。通过对不同能力层级（从Qwen3.5-9B到Claude Opus~4.6）的模型进行对比实验来验证假设。

Result: 两个关键发现：1）大纲更新能力与基础能力平缓相关：不同能力层级的模型产生的大纲更新导致的增益惊人地相似，甚至Qwen3.5-9B的更新产生的增益与Claude Opus~4.6相当；2）大纲收益能力与基础能力呈非单调关系：弱层级模型从更新的大纲中获益较少，中层级模型获益最多，而强层级模型获益少于中层级。弱层级模型低收益的原因包括：无法激活相关的大纲组件，或激活了但未能忠实遵循。

Conclusion: 研究建议：1）在任务解决智能体而非进化器上投入能力预算；2）在大纲调用和长时域指令遵循方面加强智能体训练。源代码已公开。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/a-evolve/tree/release/harness-evolution.

</details>


### [106] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRBench是一个基于真实患者电子健康记录(EHR)的自动化临床决策评估基准，通过EHR-LLM-KB交互管道构建了近100万个问答条目，涵盖诊断、治疗和预后三大任务，并对30余个LLM进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策(CDM)是真实临床工作流程的核心，但LLM在这一领域的可靠性尚不明确。现有基准测试在规模和可靠性方面存在不足，需要一个基于真实患者EHR的基准来更好地评估需要实质性生物医学知识和临床推理的实际CDM任务。

Method: EHRBench通过EHR-LLM-KB(知识库)交互管道构建，采用专门设计的LLM自动将就诊级别的EHR轨迹转换为结构化模板，然后确定性实例化为问答条目；同时应用系统性基于KB的验证和富化来过滤幻觉或歧义关系，提高可靠性。

Result: 构建了960,067个问答条目，覆盖诊断、治疗和预后三大核心推理要求任务；对30余个代表性LLM进行了基准测试，结果显示在不同设置下能力趋势一致，验证了EHRBench的可靠性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了EHRBench的可靠性，同时揭示了当前LLM向临床可靠系统迈进仍存在可操作的改进空间(gaps)。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [107] [Structure-Induced Information for Rerooting Levin Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30664)
*Jake Tuero,Michael Buro,Laurent Orseau,Levi H. S. Lelis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于学习「重根器」(rerooter)的 subgoal-based 策略树搜索方法，通过√LTS算法隐式分解问题为软子任务，避免显式生成subgoal的开销，提出聚类、启发式和混合三种重根器设计，在复杂环境中实现比传统subgoal方法更好的可扩展性和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于subgoal的策略树搜索方法需要显式生成subgoal，导致计算开销大、阻碍可扩展性。

Method: 使用学习得到的「重根器」(rerooter)通过√LTS算法隐式分解问题为软子任务，提出三种设计：(i)基于聚类的重根器利用全局状态空间结构；(ii)基于启发式的重根器利用学到的代价估计；(iii)结合两种信号的混合重根器。

Result: 所提方法可扩展至传统subgoal方法失效的复杂环境，在测试领域达到最先进的在线训练效率。

Conclusion: 基于重根器的框架通过避免显式构建和推理subgoal，实现了更低计算开销和可扩展的搜索努力分配。

Abstract: Subgoal-based policy tree search, which uses a policy to guide search, is effective for complex single-agent deterministic problems but often relies on explicit subgoal generation that can incur substantial overhead and hinders scalability. In this paper, we overcome these limitations by using a learned ``rerooter'' through the recently-introduced $\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$ algorithm. A rerooter implicitly decomposes the problem into soft subtasks. While previous work focused on the formal guarantees for given or handcrafted rerooters, in this work we propose three rerooter designs: (i) a clustering-based rerooter that exploits global state-space structure, (ii) a heuristic-based rerooter that leverages learned cost-to-go estimates, and (iii) a hybrid that combines both signals. Our framework avoids having to explicitly reconstruct and reason over generated subgoals, thereby enabling scalable allocation of search effort with significantly lower computational overhead. Empirically, our rerooting-based methods scale to complex environments where subgoal-based policy tree search fails, and achieve state-of-the-art online training efficiency on the domains tested.

</details>


### [108] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将医院机制设计重新定义为语言模型的程序综合问题，通过Medi-Sim多智能体模拟器评估可检查的规则程序，发现激励扰动可复现经典健康经济学结论，且LLM引导的进化搜索能合成消除过度编码、减少拒绝率同时保留大部分利润的多目标程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准测试将战略提供者响应固定，无法通过均衡结果评估机制设计的效果。

Method: 将医院机制设计重构为语言模型的程序综合问题，使用Medi-Sim多智能体模拟器（含编码、选择、延迟、努力、分诊5个战略渠道）对可检查的规则程序进行评分，通过激励扫描和LLM引导的进化代码搜索探索规则程序空间。

Result: 激励扫描复现了经典健康经济学结论（利润压力下的过度编码和低复杂度患者选择、测量性能与真实结果反相关的古德哈特式漂移）；单次审计可暴露压力迁移（关闭编码渠道使低复杂度选择翻倍）；合成程序消除过度编码、拒绝率减半、保留大部分利润导向基准资金。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效合成可检查的多目标机制程序，为医疗政策设计提供可解释的优化方案。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [109] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRiD是一个用于知识图谱中图结构规则发现的框架，通过将图结构规则发现重新定义为基于目标关系的离散生成过程，并采用监督预训练加强化学习微调的两阶段训练策略，直接利用不可微分的规则质量指标进行优化引导，在6个基准数据集上实现了有竞争力的知识图谱补全性能，并证明图结构规则可与链状规则形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注链状规则，忽略了图中丰富的环路、分支等图结构信息；同时计算复杂度随搜索空间呈组合增长；扩散模型等生成方法虽在其他领域成功，但因训练目标与规则学习目标不对齐，且KG规则质量评估指标不可微分，无法直接用于指导模型优化。

Method: 1）将图结构规则发现重新定义为基于目标关系的离散生成过程；2）两阶段训练：第一阶段通过监督预训练从KG元图采样的子图中捕获结构先验；第二阶段使用策略梯度优化进行强化学习微调，直接由不可微分的规则质量指标指导优化。

Result: 在6个基准数据集上实现竞争力的KG补全性能；消融实验验证了方法的高效性和鲁棒性；图结构规则可与链状规则形成互补。

Conclusion: GRiD成功解决了图结构规则挖掘的关键挑战，通过生成式框架和两阶段训练策略，有效利用不可微分指标进行优化引导，为知识图谱推理提供了更丰富的规则表示能力。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph. Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [110] [PReMISE: Policy Rubrics as Measurement Specifications for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30803)
*Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Tharindu Kumarage,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Anna Rumshisky,Pradeep Natarajan,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PReMISE框架用于审计和改进LLM评判者的评分标准(rubrics),解决开放性回答评估中评分标准模糊导致的奖励不当回答问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者广泛用于评估开放性回答，但其评分高度依赖评分标准(rubric)。模糊的评分标准可能奖励表面优秀但包含虚假信息或违背用户意图的回答。需要将评分标准视为测量规范来系统性地审计和改进。

Method: 提出PReMISE框架:(i)基于成对人类偏好数据发现策略级评分标准集合;(ii)从结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合度和对抗鲁棒性四个维度审计任意评分标准。两个修复操作:偏好排序选择和可靠性约束细化。

Result: 无任何原始评分标准源能同时满足可靠、偏好预测和对抗鲁棒。评分者间高一致性不意味着低可利用性。PRePISE是唯一在适用性、特异性和有效维度性上同时达到非平凡得分的来源。偏好排序选择将评判准确率从65.0%提升至68.6%;可靠性约束细化将可利用回答的高分率从46.4%降至36.0%。

Conclusion: PReMISE提供了系统审计和改进LLM评判评分标准的工具,两种修复操作能有效提升评判质量和对对抗攻击的抵抗能力。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended responses, but their scores depend strongly on the rubrics that condition them. A vague rubric asking for a response to be ``helpful and factual'' can reward polished answers that invent facts or violate user intent. We treat reusable rubrics as measurement specifications: changing the rubric changes the response quality measurement induced by a fixed judge. We introduce PReMISE, a framework that, given pairwise human-preference data, (i) discovers a policy-level rubric set, and (ii) audits any rubric set under LLM-judge use along four axes: structural adequacy, reliability, preference fit, and adversarial robustness. Across rubric sources no raw source is simultaneously reliable, preference-predictive, and adversarially robust; and high inter-rater agreement does not imply low exploitability. PReMISE is the only rubric source to score non-trivially on applicability, specificity, and effective dimensionality simultaneously. We contribute two audit-targeted repair operations: preference-rank selection raises judge accuracy on paired responses from $65.0\%$ to $68.6\%$, competitive with the strongest rubric-discovery baselines and leading on two of three judges in our cross-judge sweep; reliability-constrained refinement reduces the rate at which exploit responses receive high scores from $46.4\%$ to $36.0\%$ with little change in inter-judge agreement ($α{=}.531\to.519$).

</details>


### [111] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
